📖 LEARNED: Food Preservation (Level 1). Salt, smoke, dry. The big three. Salt draws water out; bacteria can't live without it.
📖 LEARNED: Reading Weather (Level 1). Red sky at night, shepherd's delight. Red sky at morning, shepherd's warning. It works.
📖 LEARNED: Animal Behavior (Level 1). Most animals flee. Give them an escape route and they take it. Cornered animals fight.
📖 LEARNED: Wire Snares (Level 1). A loop of wire on a game trail. Simple, effective, ancient. Check at dawn.
📖 LEARNED: Cordage (Level 1). Twist plant fibers into string. Dogbane, nettle, yucca — strong fibers are everywhere.
📖 LEARNED: Reading the Land (Level 1). Eat the landscape with your eyes first: green + water + sun means food grows here. Start where those three meet.
📖 LEARNED: Reading Tracks (Level 1). Fresh tracks have sharp edges. Old tracks crumble. You can tell how recently something passed.
📖 LEARNED: Field Dressing (Level 1). Break down game fast and clean. Less waste, more meat, usable parts.
📖 LEARNED: Stalk (Level 1). Move unseen. Close distance without being noticed.
